/**
 * Interactive terminal endpoints backing the UI terminal panel.
 *
 *   POST   /api/terminals               -> spawn a pty, returns { terminalId, cols, rows }
 *   GET    /api/terminals/:id/stream     -> SSE of pty output (resumable via Last-Event-ID)
 *   POST   /api/terminals/:id/input      -> write stdin { data }
 *   POST   /api/terminals/:id/resize     -> { cols, rows }
 *   DELETE /api/terminals/:id            -> kill the pty
 *
 * Transport is SSE (output) + POST (input) because this runtime has no
 * websocket server. See src/server/terminal/terminal-manager.ts.
 */
import { errorResponse } from "@/server/api-errors";
import { requireApiSession } from "@/server/auth/guards";
import { getTerminalManager } from "@/server/terminal/terminal-manager";
import { resolveConversationCwd } from "@/server/terminal/cwd";
import type { OmniHttpHandler } from "@/runtime/http/registry";
import { toNextRequest } from "./next-request";

const AUTH_SOURCE = "Terminal";

function methodNotAllowed(allow: string) {
  return Response.json(
    { error: { code: "method_not_allowed", message: "Method not allowed." } },
    { status: 405, headers: { allow } },
  );
}

async function readJsonBody(request: Request): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();
    return body && typeof body === "object" ? (body as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export const handleTerminalCreateRequest: OmniHttpHandler = async (request) => {
  if (request.method !== "POST") {
    return methodNotAllowed("POST");
  }
  const auth = await requireApiSession(toNextRequest(request), {
    source: AUTH_SOURCE,
    action: "Open terminal",
    enforceSameOrigin: true,
  });
  if (auth.response) {
    return auth.response;
  }

  try {
    const body = await readJsonBody(request);
    const conversationId = typeof body.conversationId === "string" ? body.conversationId : null;
    const cwd = await resolveConversationCwd(conversationId);
    const created = getTerminalManager().createTerminal({
      cwd,
      cols: toDimension(body.cols),
      rows: toDimension(body.rows),
    });
    return Response.json({ terminalId: created.id, cols: created.cols, rows: created.rows, cwd });

```text
/**
 * Singleton kept on the process object so it survives Next.js module
 * re-evaluation during dev HMR (mirrors the auth session cache pattern).
 */
const globalWithManager = process as NodeJS.Process & {
  __omniHarnessTerminalManager?: TerminalManager;
};

export function getTerminalManager(): TerminalManager {
  if (!globalWithManager.__omniHarnessTerminalManager) {
    const manager = new TerminalManager();
    globalWithManager.__omniHarnessTerminalManager = manager;
    const shutdown = () => manager.killAll();
    process.once("exit", shutdown);
    process.once("SIGTERM", shutdown);
    process.once("SIGINT", shutdown);
  }
  return globalWithManager.__omniHarnessTerminalManager;
}

export type { TerminalManager };

function createDbState() {
  const client = createClient({ url: `file:${dbPath}` });
  const schemaInitStart = Date.now();
  const dbReady = initializeSchema(client).then(() => {
    console.log(`[db] schema ready in ${Date.now() - schemaInitStart}ms`);
  });
  const db = drizzle(client, { schema });
  return { client, dbReady, db };
}

const processDb = process as NodeJS.Process & {
  __omniHarnessDbStates?: Map<string, ReturnType<typeof createDbState>>;
};

const dbStates = processDb.__omniHarnessDbStates ??= new Map();
let dbState = dbStates.get(dbPath);
if (!dbState) {
  dbState = createDbState();
  dbStates.set(dbPath, dbState);
}

export const dbReady = dbState.dbReady;
dbReady.catch((error) => {
  console.error("Failed to initialize database schema:", error);
  process.exit(1);
});

export const db = dbState.db;

5	
6	let mainWindow: BrowserWindow | null = null;
7	let runtimeHandle: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof startElectronOmniRuntime>> | null = null;
8	
9	async function createWindow() {
10	  const rendererAssetsDir = path.join(__dirname, "renderer");
11	  runtimeHandle = await startElectronOmniRuntime({
12	    staticDir: rendererAssetsDir,
13	  });
14	  const rendererUrl = resolveElectronRendererUrl({
15	    runtimeOrigin: runtimeHandle.origin,
16	  });
17	
18	  mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
19	    width: 1280,
20	    height: 860,
21	    minWidth: 960,
22	    minHeight: 640,
23	    webPreferences: {
24	      contextIsolation: true,
25	      nodeIntegration: false,
26	      preload: path.join(__dirname, "preload.js"),
27	    },
28	  });
29	
30	  mainWindow.on("closed", () => {
31	    mainWindow = null;
32	  });
33	  await mainWindow.loadURL(rendererUrl);
34	}
35	
36	function installNativeBridge() {
37	  ipcMain.handle("omni:native", async (event, request) => {
38	    if (!runtimeHandle) {
39	      throw new Error("OmniHarness runtime is not ready.");
40	    }
41	    return handleElectronNativeCommand(request, {
42	      runtimeOrigin: runtimeHandle.origin,
43	      senderUrl: event.senderFrame?.url ?? event.sender.getURL(),
44	      openExternal: async ({ url }) => {
45	        await shell.openExternal(url);
46	        return { ok: true };
47	      },
48	      chooseFolder: async () => {
49	        const options = {
50	          properties: ["openDirectory", "createDirectory"],
51	        } satisfies Electron.OpenDialogOptions;
52	        const result = mainWindow
53	          ? await dialog.showOpenDialog(mainWindow, options)
54	          : await dialog.showOpenDialog(options);
55	        return { path: result.canceled ? null : result.filePaths[0] ?? null };
56	      },
57	      notify: async ({ title, body }) => {
58	        if (!Notification.isSupported()) {
59	          return { ok: false };
60	        }
61	        new Notification({ title, body }).show();
62	        return { ok: true };
63	      },
64	    });
65	  });
66	}
67	
68	app.whenReady().then(async () => {
69	  installNativeBridge();
70	  await createWindow();
71	
72	  app.on("activate", () => {
73	    if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0) {
74	      void createWindow();
75	    }
76	  });
77	}).catch((error) => {
78	  dialog.showErrorBox("OmniHarness failed to start", error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
79	  app.quit();
80	});
81	
82	app.on("window-all-closed", () => {
83	  if (process.platform !== "darwin") {
84	    app.quit();
85	  }
86	});
87	
88	app.on("before-quit", (event) => {
89	  if (!runtimeHandle) {
90	    return;
91	  }
92	  event.preventDefault();
93	  const handle = runtimeHandle;
94	  runtimeHandle = null;
95	  void handle.stop().finally(() => app.exit(0));
96	});

5	
6	export interface ElectronRuntimeOptions {
7	  host?: string;
8	  port?: number;
9	  label?: string;
10	  staticDir?: string | null;
11	  createRuntime?: () => OmniRuntime;
12	  createRegistry?: () => OmniHttpRegistry;
13	  startServer?: (options: StartOmniServerOptions) => Promise<OmniServerHandle>;
14	}

16	export async function startElectronOmniRuntime(options: ElectronRuntimeOptions = {}) {
17	  const runtime = options.createRuntime?.() ?? createOmniRuntime({
18	    surface: "electron",
19	    label: options.label ?? "OmniHarness Desktop",
20	  });
21	  const registry = options.createRegistry?.() ?? createOmniRuntimeHttpRegistry();
22	  const startServerImpl = options.startServer ?? startOmniServer;
23	
24	  return startServerImpl({
25	    host: options.host ?? "127.0.0.1",
26	    port: options.port ?? 0,
27	    surface: "electron",
28	    runtime,
29	    registry,
30	    staticDir: options.staticDir ?? null,
31	  });
32	}
33	
34	export function resolveElectronRendererUrl(input: {
35	  runtimeOrigin: string;
36	  env?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
37	}) {
38	  const env = input.env ?? process.env;
39	  const configured = env.OMNI_ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL?.trim()
40	    || env.OMNI_SERVER_URL?.trim()
41	    || "";
42	  return configured || input.runtimeOrigin;
43	}

=== OmniApp ===
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import { BootShell } from "@/components/BootShell";
import type { HomeBootstrapPayload } from "@/runtime/bootstrap";
import type { RuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/types";
import { RuntimeApiProvider, createDefaultWebRuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/provider";

const HomeApp = dynamic(
  () => import("@/app/home/HomeApp").then((module) => module.HomeApp),
  {
    ssr: false,
    loading: () => <BootShell />,
  },
);

export function OmniApp({
  bootstrap,
  runtimeApis,
}: {
  bootstrap: HomeBootstrapPayload;
  runtimeApis?: RuntimeAPIs;
}) {
  const defaultRuntimeApis = useMemo(() => runtimeApis ?? createDefaultWebRuntimeAPIs(), [runtimeApis]);

  return (
    <RuntimeApiProvider apis={defaultRuntimeApis}>
      <HomeApp bootstrap={bootstrap} />
    </RuntimeApiProvider>
  );
}
=== provider ===
"use client";

import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
import type { PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import type { RuntimeAPIs } from "./types";
import { createWebRuntimeAPIs } from "./web";

const RuntimeApiContext = createContext<RuntimeAPIs | null>(null);

export function RuntimeApiProvider({
  apis,
  children,
}: PropsWithChildren<{ apis: RuntimeAPIs }>) {
  return (
    <RuntimeApiContext.Provider value={apis}>
      {children}
    </RuntimeApiContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useRuntimeAPIs() {
  const apis = useContext(RuntimeApiContext);
  if (!apis) {
    throw new Error("Runtime APIs are not available in this renderer tree.");
  }
  return apis;
}

export function createDefaultWebRuntimeAPIs() {
  return createWebRuntimeAPIs();
}

  variant?: "terminal" | "native";
  textSizeScope?: "terminal" | "conversation";
  conversationMessageTextSize?: boolean;
  className?: string;
  showTextSizeControl?: boolean;
  showPendingAssistantIndicator?: boolean;

=== src/app/api/events/route.ts (      17 lines)
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { handleEventsRequest } from "@/runtime/http/routes/events";
import { withOuterProbe } from "@/server/slow-probe";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const isSnapshot = url.searchParams.get("snapshot") === "1";
  // Skip the outer probe for the long-poll SSE stream: it is expected to
  // be long-lived and would always trip the threshold without signal.
  if (!isSnapshot) {
    return handleEventsRequest(req, { surface: "web" });
  }
  const label = `GET /api/events${url.search}`;
  return withOuterProbe(label, () => handleEventsRequest(req, { surface: "web" }));
}
=== src/app/api/agents/[name]/acp/route.ts (      10 lines)

import { handleAgentAcpRequest } from "@/runtime/http/routes/agent-detail";

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ name: string }> },
) {
  const { name } = await params;
  return handleAgentAcpRequest(req, { surface: "web", params: { name } });
}

# Password auth for the web UI and phone pairing.
# Use OMNIHARNESS_AUTH_PASSWORD for a simple local setup.
# OMNIHARNESS_AUTH_PASSWORD=change-me
#
# Prefer OMNIHARNESS_AUTH_PASSWORD_HASH if this env file may be viewed by others.
# OMNIHARNESS_AUTH_PASSWORD_HASH=

# Optional: public or LAN origin for phone pairing and remote access links.
# OMNIHARNESS_PUBLIC_ORIGIN=https://example.trycloudflare.com

# Optional: supervisor model configuration. These can also be set in Settings.
# SUPERVISOR_LLM_PROVIDER=gemini
# SUPERVISOR_LLM_MODEL=gemini-3.5-flash
# SUPERVISOR_LLM_API_KEY=
# SUPERVISOR_FALLBACK_LLM_PROVIDER=openai
# SUPERVISOR_FALLBACK_LLM_MODEL=gpt-5.4-mini
# SUPERVISOR_FALLBACK_LLM_API_KEY=

# Optional: agent provider API keys, if your local agent CLI is not already logged in.
# OPENAI_API_KEY=
# ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=
# GEMINI_API_KEY=

# Optional: avoid compiling codex-acp with host Rust. Auto downloads a prebuilt binary.
# Windows PowerShell users can set OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_INSTALL_DIR to a user bin directory.
# OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_INSTALL=auto
# OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_RELEASE_REPO=danduma/omniharness
# OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_RELEASE_TAG=codex-acp-latest
# OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_DOWNLOAD_BASE_URL=https://github.com/danduma/omniharness/releases/download/codex-acp-latest
# OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_INSTALL_DIR=/Users/you/.local/bin
# Optional fallback: run codex-acp through Docker or Podman instead.
# OMNIHARNESS_CODEX_ACP_DOCKER_IMAGE=omniharness/codex-acp:local
# OMNIHARNESS_DOCKER_BIN=podman

# Optional: generic external credential profiles for ACP workers.
# OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR=.omniharness/credential-profiles
# OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILE_CLAUDE=claude
# OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_COMMAND_CLAUDE=/Users/you/.local/bin/baton
# OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_COMMAND_ARGS_CLAUDE=["credential-profile"]

# Unified worker conversation stream (Phase 4 cutover — default ON). Set
# to 0/false/off to revert to the legacy messages-table reader path
# while the rollout settles. The flag is removed entirely in Phase 5.
# OMNI_UNIFIED_WORKER_STREAM=1

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { buildAppError } from "@/server/api-errors";
import { AUTH_SESSION_COOKIE, getAuthConfigurationError, isAuthEnabled, isAutomationAuthBypassEnabled } from "@/server/auth/config";
import type { ActiveAuthSession } from "@/server/auth/session";

const API_SESSION_CACHE_TTL_MS = 10_000;
const API_SESSION_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 128;

type ApiSessionCacheEntry = {
  session: ActiveAuthSession;
  expiresAtMs: number;
};

const processAuthGuards = process as NodeJS.Process & {
  __omniHarnessApiSessionCache?: Map<string, ApiSessionCacheEntry>;
};

function apiSessionCache() {
  return processAuthGuards.__omniHarnessApiSessionCache ??= new Map();
}

function pruneExpiredApiSessions(now: number) {
  const cache = apiSessionCache();
  for (const [cookie, entry] of cache) {
    if (entry.expiresAtMs <= now) {
      cache.delete(cookie);
    }
  }
  return cache;
}

function setCachedApiSession(cookie: string, session: ActiveAuthSession) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const cache = pruneExpiredApiSessions(now);
  cache.delete(cookie);
  cache.set(cookie, {
    session,
    expiresAtMs: now + API_SESSION_CACHE_TTL_MS,
  });

  while (cache.size > API_SESSION_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES) {
    const oldestCookie = cache.keys().next().value;
    if (!oldestCookie) {
      break;
    }
    cache.delete(oldestCookie);
  }
}

export function __resetApiSessionCacheForTests() {
  processAuthGuards.__omniHarnessApiSessionCache?.clear();
}

function jsonError(status: number, source: string, action: string, message: string) {
  return NextResponse.json({
    error: buildAppError(message, { status, source, action }),
  }, { status });
}

export function isSafeMethod(method: string) {
  return method === "GET" || method === "HEAD" || method === "OPTIONS";
}

export function isSameOriginRequest(request: NextRequest) {
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin")?.trim();
  if (!origin) {
    return true;
  }

  let parsedOrigin: string;
  try {
    parsedOrigin = new URL(origin).origin;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }

  const requestUrl = new URL(request.url);
  const allowedOrigins = new Set([requestUrl.origin]);
  const host = firstHeaderValue(request.headers.get("x-forwarded-host")) || request.headers.get("host")?.trim();
  const protocol = firstHeaderValue(request.headers.get("x-forwarded-proto")) || requestUrl.protocol.replace(/:$/, "");

```text
import { AUTH_SESSION_COOKIE } from "@/server/auth/config";

export function setSessionCookie(response: Response, tokenValue: string, expires: Date) {
  response.headers.append(
    "set-cookie",
    `${AUTH_SESSION_COOKIE}=${tokenValue}; Expires=${expires.toUTCString()}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/`
      + (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" ? "; Secure" : ""),
  );
}

export function clearSessionCookie(response: Response) {
  response.headers.append(
    "set-cookie",
    `${AUTH_SESSION_COOKIE}=; Expires=${new Date(0).toUTCString()}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/`
      + (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production" ? "; Secure" : ""),
  );
}
=== app-root.ts ===
import path from "path";

export function getAppRoot() {
  const configuredRoot = process.env.OMNIHARNESS_ROOT?.trim();
  return configuredRoot ? path.resolve(configuredRoot) : process.cwd();
}

export function getAppDataPath(...segments: string[]) {
  return path.join(getAppRoot(), ...segments);
}

function prefixStream(stream: NodeJS.ReadableStream | null, prefix: string, onLine?: (line: string) => void) {
  if (!stream) {
    return;
  }

  stream.on("data", (chunk) => {
    const text = String(chunk);
    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
    lines.forEach((line, index) => {
      if (line.length === 0 && index === lines.length - 1) {
        return;
      }
      onLine?.(line);
      process.stdout.write(`[${prefix}] ${line}\n`);
    });
  });
}

function spawnManaged(command: string, args: string[], cwd: string, prefix: string, onLine?: (line: string) => void) {
  const child = spawn(command, args, {
    cwd,
    env: process.env,
    stdio: ["inherit", "pipe", "pipe"],
  });

  prefixStream(child.stdout, prefix, onLine);

import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import process from "process";
import { resolvePnpmArgs, resolvePnpmCommand } from "./package-manager-command";

process.env.OMNIHARNESS_SERVER_MODE = "production";
const pnpmCommand = resolvePnpmCommand();

// `next start` needs a complete production build in .next. A dev/turbo run
// leaves BUILD_ID in place but rewrites routes-manifest.json without
// `dataRoutes`, which makes `next start` crash with
// "routesManifest.dataRoutes is not iterable". Validating the manifest (not just
// the presence of BUILD_ID) keeps this production entry self-contained: it can
// be the restart-control "prod" command directly, without depending on the
// first-time ./omniharness setup having run a build first.
function hasValidProductionBuild(): boolean {
  const manifestPath = path.join(process.cwd(), ".next", "routes-manifest.json");
  try {
    const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8")) as { dataRoutes?: unknown };
    return Array.isArray(manifest.dataRoutes);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

if (!hasValidProductionBuild()) {
  process.stdout.write("[start] No valid production build in .next; running `pnpm build`...\n");
  execFileSync(pnpmCommand, resolvePnpmArgs(["build"]), { stdio: "inherit" });
}

void import("./dev").catch((error) => {
  process.stderr.write(`${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
  process.exit(1);
});

function buildQuery(params: Record<string, string | null | undefined>) {
  const search = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (value != null && value !== "") {
      search.set(key, value);
    }
  }
  const value = search.toString();
  return value ? `?${value}` : "";
}

function parseBody(bodyText: string | undefined) {
  if (!bodyText) return null;
  return JSON.parse(bodyText) as unknown;
}

- NEVER EVER EVER delete the `bugdrop-screenshots` branch, locally or remotely. It is protected project history.
- NEVER create a worktree, UNLESS the user has specifically asked for it!
- NEVER delete files unless the user has specifically asked for it. If you think a file is misplaced, unrelated, or stale, that is an entirely separate request — ask first. "Looks unrelated to this task" is not authorization to delete.

- When the user gives a UUID and asks what is going on with it, treat it as an OmniHarness conversation/session lookup: check `sqlite.db`, starting with the `runs` row for that UUID, then inspect related `workers`, `messages`, `execution_events`, queued messages, and validation/plan records as needed.
- To delete all conversations and associated persisted artifacts, use `scripts/delete-conversations.sh`

Testing:
- When testing the app, use the already-running process if one exists instead of starting another server.
- The normal local app URL is `http://localhost:3035`; the compressed Next dev server is usually at `http://localhost:3050`.
- Clean up any test sessions/conversations and their associated persisted artifacts before finishing.
- For lifecycle / chaos-style regressions (reconnect, restart, FK-on-delete, plan-review leftover state), use `pnpm test:lifecycle`. Scenarios live under `tests/lifecycle/scenarios/` and drive the control plane via HTTP/SSE — no Chromium. To debug a specific reported bug, mirror it as a new scenario file.
- Read `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md` before adding new server-side state transitions. It is the spec all new code is held to.
- Worker conversation content (direct-control and supervisor-driven worker turns) lives in the unified worker stream — one append-only JSONL per worker at `app-data/run-data/<runId>/<workerId>.jsonl`. Persist new worker content through `appendWorkerEntry` in `src/server/workers/output-store.ts`; render it through `WorkerEntriesManager` and the `entries` prop on `Terminal`. Do NOT add a parallel persistence layer (a sibling JSONL, a new `messages.kind`, an in-memory cache that the frontend reconciles against the stream). See `docs/architecture/worker-conversation-stream.md`.

Lifecycle observability rules (full doc: `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md`):
- Every server-side decision (spawn, reattach, recreate, give up, refuse, delete, fail) emits a typed named event via `emitNamedEvent` from `@/server/events/named-events`. Silent early returns and bare `catch {}` are bugs.
- User-relevant failures additionally emit `error.surfaced` with a stable `code` (typed union in `named-events.ts`), `surface`, and at least one relevant subject id such as `runId`/`workerId`/`conversationId`/`accountId`. Never funnel through a blanket wrapper in `api-errors.ts`.
- All SSE frames carry an `id:`. Clients reconnect with `Last-Event-ID`; the server replays from the ring buffer or emits `stream.resync_required`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1` (anchor id in the `x-omni-last-event-id` response header).
- Dev-only event log: `GET /api/events/log?since=<id>&runId=<id>` returns the ring buffer as JSON. Use this when triaging "X didn't happen" bug reports — if the event isn't there, the server didn't do the thing, and the next step is finding the silent branch.
- Chaos is a client-side concern. Never add fault-injection code paths to server code.

Frontend i18n:
- EVERY user-facing frontend string MUST live in `shared/locales/*.json` and be rendered with the `t()` function from `@/lib/i18n`.
- NEVER hardcode user-facing JSX text, button labels, dialog titles, aria-labels, titles, placeholders, empty states, status labels, help text, error fallback text, or visible option labels directly in components.
- For every new user-facing string, add the key to `shared/locales/en.json` and every other locale file in `shared/locales/` in the same change. Use stable dotted keys like `settings.runtime.recovery` or `conversation.sidebar.search`.
- Components that render translated strings and need to update when the language changes MUST subscribe to the i18n manager by calling `useI18nSnapshot()` from `@/lib/i18n`; then render text with the existing `t("key", params?)` function. The hook is only for re-rendering; `t()` is the translation API.
- Non-React modules that produce user-visible text must still use `t("key", params?)` at the boundary where text is produced. Protocol values, storage keys, API paths, CSS classes, model ids, and database values are not translation keys unless they are displayed as copy.
- Do not pass translated strings through transactions or persist translated UI copy. Store stable ids/values and translate at render time.

React Best Practices:
- MutableRefObject is deprecated in React; use RefObject instead.
- NEVER use require() to import modules. Use import instead.
- ALWAYS centralize all state in global single source of truth Manager classes. Components/listeners subscribe to updates from Managers and use Manager methods to update data.
- Always implement a custom data manager for any data structure. NEVER use separate state variables and arrays as the source of truth.
- NEVER send data around in transactions.
- Avoid using useEffect() to update state. Prefer useCallback() or Manager methods for explicit state transitions.
- Avoid callback hell, race conditions, and infinite loops.
- NEVER store string literals or UI settings for the frontend in the .env file. Use literals in files, inline arrays, or .json files instead.
- When debugging React: if a fix did not work, think hard about whether this is a race condition. Race conditions are common in React.

## Claude subagent agent-a0562025a11f5f091: Claude tool result: toolu_019xxSogxgbKq8esGKuHTYKN · 2026-07-29T11:23:31.634Z

```text
1	# Common Runtime Multi-Surface Architecture
2	
3	## Intent
4	
5	OmniHarness should behave like one local supervision runtime with many thin
6	interfaces. The browser, standalone server, native shells, editor extensions,
7	and future surfaces should share runtime services and renderer contracts rather
8	than duplicating conversation, worker, event, settings, filesystem, or auth
9	behavior.
10	
11	This follows the useful OpenChamber pattern:
12	
13	- a shared runtime/server owns product behavior,
14	- a shared React renderer owns UI behavior,
15	- each shell supplies transport and native capabilities only.
16	
17	## Implemented Slice
18	
19	The first slice now includes:
20	
21	- `src/runtime/index.ts`: runtime lifecycle handle with named events for start,
22	  stop, and start failure.
23	- `src/runtime/bootstrap.ts`: portable bootstrap builder shared by the Next page
24	  and `/api/runtime/bootstrap`.
25	- `src/runtime/http/registry.ts`: portable Fetch-style route registry.
26	- `src/runtime/http/context.ts`: common request context builder.
27	- `src/runtime/http/adapters/next.ts`: Next route adapter for portable handlers.
28	- `src/runtime/http/server.ts`: local Node HTTP server plus
29	  `startOmniServer(...)` runtime-backed handle. It can also serve staged
30	  renderer assets for shell packaging.
31	- `src/runtime-api/types.ts`: first `RuntimeAPIs` contract.
32	- `src/runtime-api/web.ts`: browser adapter over HTTP and EventSource.
33	- `src/runtime-api/electron.ts`: Electron adapter that reuses the web HTTP/SSE
34	  adapter and adds preload-mediated native capabilities.
35	- `src/runtime-api/vscode.ts`: VS Code webview adapter over `postMessage`.
36	- `src/runtime-api/provider.tsx`: React provider for injecting runtime APIs.
37	- `src/ui/OmniApp.tsx`: renderer root wrapping the existing Home app.
38	- `src/ui/render-web.tsx`: embeddable React renderer entry used by packaged
39	  shell builds.
40	- `apps/electron`: tested Electron shell contract. The main process starts the
41	  shared runtime in-process, serves staged renderer assets from the runtime
42	  origin, and gates native commands through preload/main-process allowlists.
43	- `apps/vscode`: VS Code proof extension with HTTP proxying, SSE proxying,
44	  conversation list/start controls, and editor open-file/open-diff actions.
45	- `src/runtime/http/routes`: migrated portable route handlers for bootstrap,
46	  auth/session/login/logout/pairing, settings, accounts, agents, model
47	  discovery, notifications, plans, project memory, planning review/promote,
48	  attachments, filesystem, git, conversations, messages, queued messages,
49	  worker entries, supervisor, run lifecycle mutations, run answer/resume,
50	  event-log diagnostics, and event snapshot/SSE streaming,
51	  mounted through the shared runtime route registry and used by the existing
52	  Next route files as thin adapters.
53	
54	## Surface Responsibilities
55	
56	Web/Next:
57	
58	- builds SSR bootstrap through `buildHomeBootstrap`,
59	- renders `OmniApp`,
60	- keeps compatibility route files while routes migrate behind portable handlers.
61	
62	Standalone runtime server:
63	
64	- starts an `OmniRuntime`,
65	- serves registered portable HTTP handlers,
66	- owns clean shutdown through the returned server handle.
67	
68	VS Code:
69	
70	- connects to a configured Omni server, defaulting to `http://localhost:3035`,
71	- proxies HTTP through the extension host,
72	- proxies SSE frames through `sse:open`/`sse:close`,
73	- exposes editor capabilities through typed bridge messages.
74	
75	Electron/native:
76	
77	- starts `startOmniServer(...)` in-process through the Electron shell contract,
78	- passes `apps/electron/dist/renderer` as the static asset directory,
79	- loads the shared renderer from the runtime loopback origin,
80	- keeps native commands in preload/main-process adapters and validates the
81	  sender origin against the runtime origin,
82	- can still load a configurable renderer URL in development when
83	  `OMNI_ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL` is set.
84	
85	The packaged Electron build no longer depends on a separately running Next
86	dev/prod server for UI assets.
87	
88	## Route Inventory And Migration Order
89	
90	Current route inventory:
91	
92	| Route | Runtime owner / dependencies | Portable status |
93	| --- | --- | --- |
94	| `/api/runtime/bootstrap` | `buildRuntimeBootstrap` | Migrated |
95	| `/api/auth/session` | auth config/session/audit | Migrated |
96	| `/api/auth/login` | auth config/password/session/audit/rate-limit | Migrated |
97	| `/api/auth/logout` | auth guards/session/audit | Migrated |
98	| `/api/auth/pair` | auth config/guards/pairing/audit | Migrated |
99	| `/api/auth/pair/redeem` | auth config/pairing/session/audit | Migrated |
100	| `/api/settings` | settings table, crypto, project root canonicalization | Migrated |
101	| `/api/accounts` | accounts table | Migrated |
102	| `/api/agents` | bridge client/runtime worker snapshots | Migrated |
103	| `/api/agents/catalog` | agent catalog/configuration diagnostics | Migrated |
104	| `/api/agents/[name]` | bridge client/runtime worker detail/history | Migrated |
105	| `/api/llm-models` | model discovery | Migrated |
106	| `/api/codex-auth/status` | Codex auth reader | Migrated |
107	| `/api/notifications` | auth guards, notification preferences, web push | Migrated |
108	| `/api/projects/memory` | auth guards, memory tools, project settings, runs lookup | Migrated |
109	| `/api/plans` | plans table | Migrated |
110	| `/api/attachments` | auth guards, app-data storage, attachment metadata | Migrated |
111	| `/api/planning/[id]/review` | planning review orchestration, preferences, live updates | Migrated |
112	| `/api/planning/[id]/promote` | planning promotion, live updates | Migrated |
113	| `/api/fs` | auth guards, filesystem directory listing/scope checks | Migrated |
114	| `/api/fs/files` | auth guards, project file listing/read APIs | Migrated |
115	| `/api/git` | git status/workspace mutation, project config, run recovery | Migrated |
116	| `/api/conversations` | supervisor watchdog, conversation creation, attachments, git workspace policy | Migrated |
117	| `/api/conversations/[id]/messages` | send message, attachments, busy-message actions | Migrated |
118	| `/api/messages` | messages table compatibility path | Migrated |
119	| `/api/conversations/[id]/queued-messages/[messageId]` | queued message cancel/send-now | Migrated |
120	| `/api/runs/[id]` | run mutation/delete/archive/recovery, bridge cancellation, output compaction | Migrated |
121	| `/api/runs/[id]/answer` | clarification answer/resume, messages, live updates | Migrated |
122	| `/api/runs/[id]/resume` | supervisor resume, live updates | Migrated |
123	| `/api/workers/[workerId]/entries` | worker/run auth visibility, worker output stream | Migrated |
124	| `/api/supervisor` | credits, conversation creation, supervisor watchdog | Migrated |
125	| `/api/events/log` | named-event ring buffer diagnostics | Migrated |
126	| `/api/events` | database snapshot, live worker snapshots, bridge polling, SSE replay/resync | Migrated |
127	
128	Routes should move gradually from Next-only handlers to portable handlers. Start
129	with low-risk read paths, then mutation paths, then lifecycle/streaming paths:
130	
131	1. `/api/auth/session` - migrated
132	2. `/api/settings` - migrated
133	3. `/api/accounts` - migrated
134	4. `/api/agents` - migrated
135	5. `/api/agents/catalog` - migrated
136	6. `/api/agents/[name]` - migrated
137	7. `/api/llm-models` - migrated
138	8. `/api/codex-auth/status` - migrated
139	9. `/api/auth/login` - migrated
140	10. `/api/auth/logout` - migrated
141	11. `/api/notifications` - migrated
142	12. remaining auth pairing routes - migrated
143	13. project memory and plans - migrated
144	14. attachments - migrated
145	15. filesystem routes - migrated
146	16. git routes - migrated
147	17. conversations and messages - migrated
148	18. run lifecycle routes - migrated
149	19. worker entries - migrated
150	20. supervisor - migrated
151	21. event log - migrated
152	22. `/api/events` - migrated
153	
154	`/api/events` migrated last because it carries SSE ids, replay,
155	`stream.resync_required`, snapshot markers, and lifecycle diagnostics.
156	
157	## Direct Frontend Fetch/SSE Owners
158	
159	The current renderer still contains direct fetch/SSE usage inside existing home
160	managers and hooks. New shell-aware work should add methods to `RuntimeAPIs`
161	first, then have managers consume those methods through `RuntimeApiProvider`.
162	
163	The first proven `RuntimeAPIs` slice covers:
164	
165	- bootstrap,
166	- event streaming and event-log fetch,
167	- conversation create/send,
168	- worker entries,
169	- settings load/save,
170	- Electron native open-external/folder/notification commands,
171	- editor open-file/open-diff.
172	
173	Current direct browser fetch/SSE owners still to migrate behind `RuntimeAPIs`:
174	
175	- `src/app/home/useHomeQueries.ts`: auth session, settings, agent catalog, and
176	  project-file queries.
177	- `src/app/home/useHomeMutations.ts`: attachments, auth login/logout/pair
178	  redeem, settings, run mutations, conversations, messages, queued messages,
179	  planning review/promote, and agent history.
180	- `src/app/home/LiveEventConnectionManager.ts`: `/api/events` SSE plus
181	  persisted snapshot polling.
182	- `src/app/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts`: worker entry stream fetches.
183	- `src/app/home/GitWorkspaceManager.ts`: git workspace status/mutation.
184	- `src/app/home/ProjectMemoryPanelManager.ts`: project memory list/read/write.
185	- `src/app/home/ConversationNotificationManager.ts`: notification subscribe,
186	  permission, and unsubscribe requests.
187	- `src/components/PairDeviceDialog.tsx`: pair-token creation/status polling.
188	- `src/components/FileAttachmentPickerDialog.tsx`,
189	  `src/components/FolderPickerDialog.tsx`, `src/components/home/FileViewerPanel.tsx`,
190	  and `src/app/home/ComposerContainer.tsx`: filesystem and file-content
191	  requests.
192	- `src/components/settings/ModelProfileForm.tsx`: Codex auth status fetch.
193	
194	These are compatibility callers. New shell-aware work should extend
195	`RuntimeAPIs` before adding more direct fetches.
196	
197	## Large File Split Risks
198	
199	Files at or above roughly 1200 lines need split planning before major new
200	behavior lands:
201	
202	- `src/server/supervisor/index.ts`
203	- `src/components/Terminal.tsx`
204	- `src/server/agent-runtime/manager.ts`
205	- `src/app/home/utils.ts`
206	
207	Near-threshold files that should not absorb unrelated behavior:
208	
209	- `src/server/supervisor/observer.ts`
210	- `src/components/home/ConversationMain.tsx`
211	- `src/server/workers/output-store.ts`
212	- `src/app/home/HomeApp.tsx`
213	- `src/app/home/useHomeMutations.ts`
214	- `src/app/api/events/route.ts`
215	
216	## Observability Rules
217	
218	Runtime and server-side shell decisions must emit typed named events with
219	`emitNamedEvent`. User-visible failures must also surface through
220	`error.surfaced` with stable codes. SSE behavior must preserve:
221	
222	- `id:` on event frames,
223	- `Last-Event-ID`/`lastEventId` resume,
224	- `/api/events?snapshot=1` bootstrap,
225	- `stream.resync_required`,
226	- `/api/events/log` diagnostics.
227	
228	## File Size Watch List
229	
230	Files already large enough to split before adding major new behavior:
231	
232	- `src/app/api/events/route.ts`
233	- `src/app/home/HomeApp.tsx`
234	
235	Do not add parallel persistence for worker streams, conversations, messages,
236	queued messages, plans, validation records, or events.
237

## Claude subagent agent-a0562025a11f5f091: Claude tool result: toolu_0135LNJ9wfFEYqYbXFs6n2PD · 2026-07-29T11:23:38.104Z

```text
"use client";

import React from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { OmniApp } from "@/ui/OmniApp";
import { createElectronRuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/electron";
import { createWebRuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/web";
import type { HomeBootstrapPayload } from "@/runtime/bootstrap";

function createRuntimeApis() {
  if (typeof window !== "undefined" && window.omniElectron) {
    return createElectronRuntimeAPIs();
  }
  return createWebRuntimeAPIs();
}

async function mount() {
  const root = document.getElementById("root");
  if (!root) {
    return;
  }

  root.textContent = t("boot.loading.message");
  try {
    const runtimeApis = createRuntimeApis();
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const bootstrap = await runtimeApis.bootstrap.load({
      selectedRunId: params.get("run"),
      draftProjectPath: params.get("project"),
      pairToken: params.get("pair"),
    }) as HomeBootstrapPayload;
    createRoot(root).render(<OmniApp bootstrap={bootstrap} runtimeApis={runtimeApis} />);
  } catch (error) {
    root.textContent = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  }
}

void mount();

import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { buildRuntimeBootstrap } from "@/runtime/bootstrap";
import type { HomeBootstrapPayload } from "@/runtime/bootstrap";

export type { HomeBootstrapPayload };

type PageSearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;

export async function buildHomeBootstrap(searchParams: PageSearchParams = {}): Promise<HomeBootstrapPayload> {
  const requestHeaders = await headers();
  return buildRuntimeBootstrap({ searchParams, requestHeaders, includeInitialData: false });
}
=== layout.tsx ===
import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import { Geist, Geist_Mono } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";
import "./fx.css";
import { Providers } from "./providers";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { BugDropBootstrap } from "@/components/BugDropBootstrap";
import { PwaBootstrap } from "@/components/PwaBootstrap";

const geistSans = Geist({
  variable: "--font-geist-sans",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

const geistMono = Geist_Mono({
  variable: "--font-geist-mono",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

const lightThemeColor = "#ffffff";
const darkThemeColor = "#0b0d10";

const themeBootstrapScript = `
(function() {
  var lightThemeColor = "${lightThemeColor}";
  var darkThemeColor = "${darkThemeColor}";
  function applyThemeColor(themeMode) {
    var themeColorMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    if (themeColorMeta) {
      themeColorMeta.setAttribute("content", themeMode === "night" ? darkThemeColor : lightThemeColor);
    }
  }
  try {
    var themeMode = window.localStorage.getItem("omni-theme-mode");
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", themeMode === "night");
    document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = themeMode === "night" ? "dark" : "light";
    applyThemeColor(themeMode);
  } catch (error) {
    document.documentElement.style.colorScheme = "light";
    applyThemeColor("day");
  }
})();
`;

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  applicationName: "OmniHarness",
  title: "OmniHarness",
  description: "Supervised Multi-Agent CLI Coding Orchestrator",
  manifest: "/manifest.webmanifest",
  appleWebApp: {
    capable: true,
    title: "OmniHarness",
    statusBarStyle: "default",
  },
  formatDetection: {
    telephone: false,
  },
  other: {
    "apple-mobile-web-app-capable": "yes",
  },
  icons: {
    icon: [
      {
        url: "/icons/favicon-v2.png",
        sizes: "64x64",
        type: "image/png",
      },
      {
        url: "/icons/icon-192-v2.png",
        sizes: "192x192",
        type: "image/png",
      },
      {
        url: "/icons/icon-512-v2.png",
        sizes: "512x512",
        type: "image/png",
      },
    ],
    apple: [
      {
        url: "/icons/apple-touch-icon-v2.png",
        sizes: "180x180",
        type: "image/png",
      },
    ],
  },
};

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  themeColor: lightThemeColor,
  colorScheme: "light dark",
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <script
          id="omni-theme-bootstrap"
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: themeBootstrapScript }}
        />
      </head>
      <body
        className={`${geistSans.variable} ${geistMono.variable} font-sans antialiased`}
      >
        <BugDropBootstrap />
        <PwaBootstrap />
        <Providers>
          <TooltipProvider>{children}</TooltipProvider>
        </Providers>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { appearancePreferencesManager } from "@/app/home/AppearancePreferencesManager";
import { fileViewerPanelManager } from "@/components/component-state-managers";
import { i18nManager } from "@/lib/i18n";

function makeQueryClient() {
  return new QueryClient();
}

const queryClient = makeQueryClient();

export function Providers({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    appearancePreferencesManager.hydrateFromLocalStorage();
    fileViewerPanelManager.hydrateFromLocalStorage();
    void i18nManager.hydrateAsync();
  }, []);

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

## Claude subagent agent-a0562025a11f5f091: Claude · 2026-07-29T11:26:38.220Z

````text
I have a complete picture. Here's the architecture map.

---

# OmniHarness — Architecture Map for a Runner/Interface Split

**TL;DR: the split is already ~70% designed and partially built.** There is a transport-agnostic route registry (`src/runtime/http/`), a client-side transport abstraction (`src/runtime-api/`), a standalone Node HTTP server, and working Electron + VSCode shells. A design doc (`docs/architecture/common-runtime-multi-surface.md`) explicitly states the intent. The remaining work is concentrated in a handful of specific places, listed in §10.

---

## 1. Directory structure & sizes

Total `src/`: **~94,700 LOC** across ~460 TS/TSX files.

| Area | Files | LOC | Role |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src/server/` | 190 | 48,770 | All backend domain logic. **The runner.** |
| `src/components/` | 74 | 16,848 | React UI |
| `src/app/` | 108 | 16,103 | Next App Router: 49 API routes + `src/app/home/` (51 files, 15,299 LOC of client-side managers/hooks) |
| `src/runtime/` | 45 | 6,998 | **Portable HTTP layer** (registry, routes, standalone server) |
| `src/lib/` | 34 | 4,935 | Isomorphic helpers |
| `src/runtime-api/` | 5 | 578 | **Client transport abstraction** (web/electron/vscode) |
| `src/ui/` | 2 | 71 | `OmniApp.tsx` renderer root + `render-web.tsx` non-Next mount |
| `src/vscode-extension/` | 1 | — | `bridge.ts` (HTTP+SSE proxy) |
| `src/shims/` | 1 | — | empty `encoding` shim for webpack/turbopack |

Biggest single modules:
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/agent-runtime/manager.ts` — **2,783 lines** (ACP agent process manager)
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/supervisor/index.ts` — 2,093 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/components/Terminal.tsx` — 2,553 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/app/home/HomeApp.tsx` — 1,697 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/app/home/utils.ts` — 1,515 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/lib/agent-output.ts` — 1,460 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/supervisor/observer.ts` — 1,386 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/app/home/useHomeMutations.ts` — 1,269 lines
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/events.ts` — 958 lines (SSE)
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/runs.ts` — 911 lines

Outside `src/`: `apps/electron/`, `apps/vscode/`, `scripts/` (40 files, incl. `dev.ts` process orchestrator), `shared/locales/*.json` (9 locales), `tests/` (27 subdirs incl. `lifecycle/`, `runtime/`, `runtime-api/`, `electron/`, `vscode/`).

---

## 2. Frontend↔backend transports (complete inventory)

**Only two transports exist: HTTP fetch (JSON) and SSE. No WebSockets, no socket.io, no tRPC, no server actions** (`grep '"use server"'` returns zero hits).

### 2a. Next.js API routes — all 49, all thin adapters

Every file in `src/app/api/**/route.ts` delegates to `src/runtime/http/routes/*`. Two adapter styles:
- `adaptOmniHandlerToNext(handler, { surface: "web" })` (`src/runtime/http/adapters/next.ts`)
- inline `async function GET(req, {params})` that awaits `params` and calls the portable handler

Canonical registry: **`/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/runtime/http/routes/index.ts`** (`createOmniRuntimeHttpRegistry()`, lines 49–113) — this is the single source of truth, listing 62 method+path pairs:

```
GET    /api/runtime/bootstrap
GET|DELETE /api/auth/session       POST /api/auth/login    POST /api/auth/logout
GET|POST /api/auth/pair            POST /api/auth/pair/redeem
GET|POST /api/settings
GET|POST|OPTIONS /api/integrations/claude-model-gateway
GET|POST /api/accounts             PATCH|DELETE /api/accounts/:id
POST   /api/accounts/:id/status
GET    /api/agents                 GET  /api/agents/:name
POST   /api/agents/:name/elicitation    POST /api/agents/:name/permission
POST   /api/agents/:name/acp
GET    /api/agents/catalog         POST /api/agents/prewarm-worker
POST   /api/llm-models             GET  /api/codex-auth/status
GET|POST|DELETE /api/notifications
GET    /api/plans
GET|POST /api/projects/memory
GET|POST /api/attachments
POST   /api/planning/:id/review    POST /api/planning/:id/promote
GET    /api/fs                     GET  /api/fs/files
POST   /api/git
GET    /api/messages
POST   /api/conversations          POST /api/conversations/:id/messages
PATCH|DELETE /api/conversations/:id/queued-messages/:messageId
POST   /api/conversations/:id/queued-messages/interrupt-next
POST   /api/conversations/:id/queued-messages/:messageId/interrupt
GET    /api/conversations/:id/transcript
GET    /api/workers/:workerId/entries
GET    /api/events/log
GET    /api/events                 <-- SSE + snapshot
POST   /api/supervisor
PATCH|POST|DELETE /api/runs/:id
POST   /api/runs/:id/answer        POST /api/runs/:id/resume
GET    /api/external-sessions
POST   /api/terminals              GET  /api/terminals/:id/stream   <-- SSE
POST   /api/terminals/:id/input    POST /api/terminals/:id/resize
DELETE /api/terminals/:id
```

Note: `src/app/api/events/route.ts` exists as a file but there is **no** corresponding registry entry mismatch — everything is covered. `/api/agents/catalog` has a static/dynamic collision risk with `/api/agents/:name` in the custom registry's matcher (`registry.ts` checks exact routes first, so it's fine).

### 2b. SSE endpoints (2)

1. **`GET /api/events`** — `src/runtime/http/routes/events.ts:814-958`. The central live-state stream. Frames: `update` (full snapshot JSON), typed named-event kinds, `stream.resync_required`, `update_error`, `: heartbeat`. Every frame carries `id:`; resume via `Last-Event-ID` header or `?lastEventId=`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1[&persisted=1]`, anchor id returned in `x-omni-last-event-id`, plus `x-omni-snapshot-checksum`.
2. **`GET /api/terminals/:id/stream`** — `src/runtime/http/routes/terminals.ts:93-150`. PTY output; also resumable via `Last-Event-ID`. Header comment explicitly says "SSE + POST because this runtime has no websocket server."

### 2c. Client-side transport layer (already abstracted)

- `src/runtime-api/types.ts` — the `RuntimeAPIs` interface (bootstrap, events.open/fetchLog, conversations, workers, settings, optional `native`, optional `editor`)
- `src/runtime-api/web.ts` — **already supports `baseUrl` and injectable `fetchImpl`/`EventSourceImpl`** (`WebRuntimeApiOptions`). This is your remote-runner client, ~90% done.
- `src/runtime-api/electron.ts` — reuses web adapter + preload IPC for native
- `src/runtime-api/vscode.ts` — `postMessage` bridge; extension host proxies HTTP and SSE to a configured `serverUrl` (default `http://localhost:3035`)
- `src/runtime-api/provider.tsx` — React context (`useRuntimeAPIs()`)

### 2d. Direct `fetch()` calls that bypass `RuntimeAPIs` (the migration debt)

29 files still call `/api/...` directly. Full list of literal paths captured; the owning files are:

`src/app/home/`: `useHomeQueries.ts`, `useHomeMutations.ts` (1,269 lines — the big one), `LiveEventConnectionManager.ts`, `WorkerEntriesManager.ts`, `EventStreamStateManager.ts`, `GitWorkspaceManager.ts`, `ProjectMemoryPanelManager.ts`, `ConversationNotificationManager.ts`, `ConversationTranscriptManager.ts`, `ClaudeModelGatewayManager.ts`, `PlanningReviewPreferencesManager.ts`, `useRunSelectionEffects.ts`, `useConversationActions.ts`, `useQueuedMessageMutations.ts`, `upload-attachments.ts`, `HomeApp.tsx`, `ComposerContainer.tsx`, `ExternalSessionsPicker.tsx`

`src/components/`: `InteractiveTerminal.tsx` (xterm + `new EventSource('/api/terminals/.../stream')`), `Terminal.tsx`, `FileAttachmentPickerDialog.tsx`, `FolderPickerDialog.tsx`, `PairDeviceDialog.tsx`, `home/FileViewerPanel.tsx`, `home/UserInputMessage.tsx`, `settings/AgentsSettingsPanel.tsx`, `settings/ModelProfileForm.tsx`

This list is also enumerated (and acknowledged as debt) in `docs/architecture/common-runtime-multi-surface.md:173-195`.

---

## 3. `src/server/` — long-running processes and state

The system runs as **two Node processes today**, not one:

### Process A: the "bridge" / agent runtime (port 7800)
- Entry: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/scripts/agent-runtime.ts` → `createAgentRuntimeServer()` in `src/server/agent-runtime/http.ts:69`
- Holds a single `new AgentRuntimeManager(...)` (`src/server/agent-runtime/manager.ts:1210`)
- State: `agents: Map<string, AgentRecord>`, `chunkSubscribers: Map<string, Set<cb>>`, `WorkerPool`, `pendingAgentReaps: Map<string, Timeout>`, `reapSweepTimer`, `resourcePressureTimer`, `MemoryTracer`
- Spawns and owns **ACP child processes** (claude-agent-acp, codex-acp, gemini, opencode) over stdio JSON-RPC via `@agentclientprotocol/sdk`
- Client side: `src/server/bridge-client/index.ts`, `BRIDGE_URL = process.env.OMNIHARNESS_BRIDGE_URL || "http://127.0.0.1:7800"`. **30 modules import it.**
- Lifecycle managed by `scripts/dev.ts` (spawns it, `.omniharness/bridge.lock.json` lockfile, `src/server/dev/managed-bridge.ts`, `src/server/dev/bridge-lock.ts`)

### Process B: the Next.js server — holds the rest
Started by `src/instrumentation.ts` (`register()`, nodejs runtime only):
1. `await dbReady` (SQLite schema init)
2. `ensureSupervisorRuntimeStarted()` → `src/server/supervisor/runtime-watchdog.ts`: **`setInterval` every 15s** doing `syncRunningSupervision()`, wake rehydration, output compaction, stuck-worker reaping, bridge session sync
3. `ensureClaudeModelGatewayStartedAtBoot()` + shutdown handlers → `src/server/integrations/claude-model-gateway/process-manager.ts` **spawns a `cliproxyapi` child process**

Other in-process singletons and child-process owners inside the Next server:
- **`src/server/terminal/terminal-manager.ts`** — `node-pty` PTYs. Singleton stashed on `process.__omniHarnessTerminalManager` (line 349) to survive HMR. Holds per-session ring buffers (256 KB), subscriber sets, idle reaper (`setInterval` 10s), 30-min idle timeout. Registers `process.once('exit'|'SIGTERM'|'SIGINT')` handlers.
- **`src/server/events/named-events.ts`** — module-level **500-entry ring buffer** (`const ring: BufferedEntry[]`, `let cursor = 0`, line 547-549). This is the SSE replay backbone.
- **`src/server/events/live-updates.ts`** — module-level `Set<listener>` + `notificationVersion` counter. The SSE loop parks on `waitForEventStreamNotification()`; mutation paths call `notifyEventStreamSubscribers()`. **Purely same-process.**
- **`src/server/db/index.ts:954-958`** — DB client map on `process.__omniHarnessDbStates`
- **`src/server/auth/guards.ts:14-19`** — API session cache on `process.__omniHarnessApiSessionCache` (10s TTL, 128 entries)
- **`src/server/session-providers/process-store.ts:35`** — `const liveProcesses = new Map<string, ProcessHandle>()` for spawned "process sessions"
- `src/server/supervisor/index.ts` uses `spawnSync`; `src/server/git/command.ts`, `src/server/git/auto-commit.ts`, `src/server/external-sessions/running.ts`, `src/server/accounts/migration.ts`, `src/server/restart-control.ts`, `src/server/process-ownership.ts` all shell out.

---

## 4. Persistence

**SQLite** via `@libsql/client` + `drizzle-orm` (also `better-sqlite3` present).
- Path: `src/server/db/index.ts:6` → `getAppDataPath('sqlite.db')`; root from `OMNIHARNESS_ROOT` or `process.cwd()` (`src/server/app-root.ts`). Override with `OMNIHARNESS_DB_PATH`.
- Live DB in repo root is **~95 MB** + a 4 MB WAL.
- Schema: `src/server/db/schema.ts` — 30 tables: `plans`, `runs`, `workers`, `process_sessions`, `worker_counters`, `messages`, `conversation_read_markers`, `queued_conversation_messages`, `recovery_incidents`, `supervisor_scheduled_wakes`, `accounts`, `account_secrets`, `worker_credential_allocations`, `worker_token_usage`, `account_usage_snapshots`, `credit_events`, `settings`, `auth_sessions`, `notification_subscriptions`, `auth_pair_tokens`, `auth_events`, `plan_items`, `clarifications`, `worker_assignments`, `execution_events`, `supervisor_interventions`, `planning_review_runs`, `planning_review_rounds`, `planning_review_findings`, `artifact_streams`.
- `drizzle.config.ts` at repo root.

**File-based storage** (this is the part that makes "remote runner" nontrivial):
- **Worker conversation streams**: append-only JSONL, one per worker, at `<projectPath>/.omniharness/run-data/<runId>/<workerId>.jsonl` (`src/server/workers/output-store.ts`, `src/server/artifacts/project-root.ts`). Falls back to `<appData>/run-data/<runId>`. SQLite stores only the relative path.
- Attachments: `getAppDataPath("attachments")` (repo `attachments/`, 25 entries)
- Per-project scoped CLI homes: `<cwd>/.omniharness/cli-home/<agent>/`
- Per-account CLI homes: `getAppDataPath("account-cli-homes/<cliType>/<accountId>")`
- Credential profiles: `<root>/.omniharness/credential-profiles`
- Auth key: `getAppDataPath(".omniharness-auth.key")`; settings encryption key: `~/.omniharness/settings.key` (`src/server/settings/crypto.ts`)
- Claude gateway managed files: `~/.omniharness/cliproxyapi`
- Diagnostics: `.omniharness/memory-trace.jsonl`, `.omniharness/bridge-validation-failures.log`, `.omniharness/*.log`, `.omniharness/bridge.lock.json`
- Project memory: `<projectPath>/.omniharness/memory/`
- `config/accounts.yml`, `shared/locales/*.json`

**Settings** live in the `settings` SQLite table, encrypted with a local key file. VAPID web-push keypair is generated once and stored there too (`src/server/notifications/web-push.ts`).

---

## 5. Real-time streams

### Terminal output — two *different* things named "terminal"

1. **`src/components/Terminal.tsx` (2,553 lines) is NOT a PTY.** It is the agent-conversation renderer. It takes a `entries: WorkerEntry[]` prop and renders markdown/tool-calls/thoughts. Data arrives via:
   - `src/app/home/WorkerEntriesManager.ts` (830 lines) — paginated `GET /api/workers/:workerId/entries?afterSeq=|beforeSeq=|limit=`
   - plus the `/api/events` SSE `update` snapshots
   - It imports `WorkerEntry` from `@/server/workers/entries-types` and helpers from `@/lib/agent-output`.

2. **`src/components/InteractiveTerminal.tsx` (160 lines) IS the PTY**, using `@xterm/xterm` + `@xterm/addon-fit`:
   - `POST /api/terminals` → `{terminalId, cols, rows}`
   - `new EventSource('/api/terminals/:id/stream')` for output
   - `POST /api/terminals/:id/input` for stdin, `/resize`, `DELETE` to kill

### Event stream mechanics (`src/runtime/http/routes/events.ts`)
A hybrid **push + long-poll-with-parking** loop:
- `src/app/home/LiveEventConnectionManager.ts` (449 lines) owns the `EventSource`, reconnect, and `Last-Event-ID` bookkeeping
- `src/app/home/EventStreamStateManager.ts` + `EventStreamSnapshotCacheManager.ts` reduce frames into client state
- Server loop: drain ring buffer → build full snapshot payload → race against `RUNTIME_AGENT_GRACE_MS` (falls back to persisted-only payload, then upgrades) → emit `update` if the serialized payload changed → park on `waitForEventStreamNotification(STREAM_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS, version)` → heartbeat on timeout
- Snapshot markers are allocated from the same ring so SSE ids stay monotonic across `update` frames and named events

**No polling fallback other than the snapshot refetch; no WebSockets anywhere.**

---

## 6. Shared client/server code → future shared package

Truly isomorphic today (`src/lib/`, 4,935 LOC, zero Node builtins, zero `@/server` runtime imports):
- `agent-output.ts` (1,460) — **the big one**; type-only import of `WorkerEntryChannel|WorkerEntryType` from `@/server/workers/entries-types`
- `worker-terminal-processes.ts`, `claude-session-model.ts`, `claude-model-gateway.ts`, `chat-attachments.ts`, `conversation-workers.ts`, `acp/elicitation-schema.ts`, `app-errors.ts`, `run-recovery-state.ts`, `conversations.ts`, `worker-terminal-messages.ts`, `project-file-links.ts`, `syntax-highlighting.ts`, `project-paths.ts`, `i18n-core.ts`/`i18n.ts`, `git-workspace.ts`, `runtime-resource-settings.ts`, `superseded-entries.ts`, `worker-stuck-events.ts`, `use-manager-snapshot.ts`, `conversation-state.ts`, `state-manager.ts`, `pwa.ts`, `local-pairing-token.ts`, `project-scope.ts`, `commit-workflow.ts`, `mentions.ts`, `run-status.ts`, `conversation-visuals.ts`, `plan-path.ts`, `planning-output.ts`, `utils.ts`, `auth-constants.ts`

Type contracts that **live in `src/server/` but are imported by client components** — these must move into the shared package:
| File | Imported by |
|---|---|
| `src/server/workers/entries-types.ts` | `components/Terminal.tsx:19`, `components/WorkerCard.tsx:16`, `lib/agent-output.ts:1` |
| `src/server/planning/review-preferences.ts` | `components/PlanningReviewControls.tsx:12`, `components/PlanningArtifactsPanel.tsx:14`, `app/home/useHomeMutations.ts:19` |
| `src/server/events/named-events.ts` | (types) `src/runtime/http/registry.ts`, `src/runtime/http/context.ts`, `src/runtime/index.ts` |

Also shared-by-nature:
- `src/app/home/types.ts` (435 lines) — `EventStreamState`, `SettingsResponse`, `AuthSessionResponse`; already re-exported through `src/runtime/bootstrap.ts`
- `src/runtime-api/types.ts` — the `RuntimeAPIs` contract
- `shared/locales/*.json` — 9 locale files, already outside `src/`

**Caveat:** `src/server/workers/entries-types.ts` is a pure-types file today (only import is the file header comment referencing bridge-client), but `src/server/planning/review-preferences.ts` imports `zod` — fine for a shared package.

---

## 7. Existing separation hints (there are many)

| Hint | Location |
|---|---|
| **Design doc for exactly this** | `docs/architecture/common-runtime-multi-surface.md` — 237 lines, route-by-route migration table, all rows "Migrated" |
| **Portable route registry** | `src/runtime/http/registry.ts` — Fetch-`Request`/`Response`, own dynamic `:param` matcher, framework-free |
| **Standalone Node HTTP server** | `src/runtime/http/server.ts` — `startOmniHttpServer()` / `startOmniServer()`, optional `staticDir` for serving a built SPA |
| **Runtime lifecycle handle** | `src/runtime/index.ts` — `createOmniRuntime({surface, label, hooks})`, emits `runtime.started`/`stopped`/`start_failed` |
| **Non-Next SPA entry** | `src/ui/render-web.tsx` — `createRoot(...).render(<OmniApp bootstrap runtimeApis/>)`, no Next |
| **`/api/runtime/bootstrap`** | HTTP equivalent of the SSR bootstrap, so a non-Next shell can hydrate |
| **Electron shell** | `apps/electron/main.ts`, `apps/electron/src/runtime.ts`, `preload.ts`, `src/native-bridge.ts`, `scripts/build.mjs` |
| **VSCode shell** | `apps/vscode/`, `src/vscode-extension/bridge.ts`, `src/runtime-api/vscode.ts` — already talks to a **remote** `serverUrl` |
| **Base-URL support in the web client** | `src/runtime-api/web.ts` — `WebRuntimeApiOptions.baseUrl` |
| **Env vars for remote endpoints** | `OMNI_SERVER_URL`, `OMNI_ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL`, `OMNIHARNESS_BRIDGE_URL`, `OMNIHARNESS_PUBLIC_ORIGIN`, `OMNIHARNESS_AGENT_RUNTIME_HOST/PORT`, `OMNIHARNESS_WEB_HOST`, `OMNIHARNESS_ROOT`, `OMNIHARNESS_DB_PATH`, `OMNIHARNESS_RUNTIME_DATA_DIR`, `OMNIHARNESS_APPDATA_DIR` |
| Multi-process orchestrator | `scripts/dev.ts` (web + bridge + compression proxy), `scripts/start.ts`, `controller.sh`, `omni`, `omni.sh`, `start.sh` |
| Tests already scoped by surface | `tests/runtime/`, `tests/runtime-api/`, `tests/electron/`, `tests/vscode/`, `tests/lifecycle/` (drives control plane over HTTP/SSE, no browser) |

**What is NOT there:** no `server.js` custom Next server, no `output: "standalone"` in `next.config.ts`, no `middleware.ts`, no `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` API-base env var.

`next.config.ts` contents: `allowedDevOrigins` (3 tunnel hostnames), `htmlLimitedBots: /.*/ `, one rewrite `/session/:runId → /?run=:runId`, and turbopack/webpack aliasing of `encoding` to `src/shims/empty-encoding.ts`.

---

## 8. Auth model

Cookie session + optional password + QR pairing. All server-side, no third-party IdP.

- Cookie `omni_session` (`src/server/auth/config.ts:6`), `HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/`, `Secure` in production — set in `src/runtime/http/routes/cookies.ts`
- Opaque token (id.secret) hashed into `auth_sessions` table; idle 30d / absolute 90d
- Methods: `password_login` | `qr_pair` (`src/server/auth/session.ts:14`)
- Password: [REDACTED] or `OMNIHARNESS_AUTH_PASSWORD_HASH` (argon2 via `@node-rs/argon2`); key file `.omniharness-auth.key`
- Pairing: `src/server/auth/pairing.ts` + `auth_pair_tokens`; QR flow in `src/components/PairDeviceDialog.tsx`, `?pair=<token>` URL param handled in `src/runtime/bootstrap.ts`
- Enforcement: `requireApiSession(...)` in `src/server/auth/guards.ts`, called in ~37 places across `src/runtime/http/routes/*` with `enforceSameOrigin: true` on all mutations
- Rate limiting: `src/server/auth/rate-limit.ts`; audit trail: `src/server/auth/audit.ts` + `auth_events` table
- Bypasses: `OMNIHARNESS_E2E_BYPASS_AUTH`, `OMNIHARNESS_TEST_BYPASS_AUTH`, `isAutomationAuthBypassEnabled()`
- **No CORS headers anywhere.** `grep -i "access-control-allow"` over `src/` returns nothing.

---

## 9. Next-specific server features vs plain React

**Very little Next-specific server surface.** Effectively three things:

1. **One server component tree**: `src/app/layout.tsx` (+ `next/font/google` Geist) and `src/app/page.tsx`. `page.tsx` is 11 lines: it awaits `buildHomeBootstrap(searchParams)` and renders `<OmniApp bootstrap/>`. `src/app/home/bootstrap.server.ts` is the only user of `next/headers`, and it calls `buildRuntimeBootstrap({ includeInitialData: false })` — so **SSR currently fetches almost nothing**; the client re-bootstraps.
2. **`src/instrumentation.ts`** — the boot hook for the supervisor watchdog + gateway.
3. **`next/server`'s `NextRequest`** — used by `src/server/auth/guards.ts`, `src/server/auth/session.ts`, `src/server/api-errors.ts`. The portable routes bridge with a 9-line shim: `src/runtime/http/routes/next-request.ts` (`new NextRequest(url, {method, headers, signal})`).

Everything else is plain client React: 61 files with `"use client"`; `HomeApp` is loaded via `next/dynamic({ ssr: false })` behind a `<BootShell/>`. State is TanStack Query + hand-rolled Manager singletons (`src/lib/state-manager.ts` + `useManagerSnapshot`); **zustand is in package.json but unused**.

`next/image` is used in 5 components (`Terminal.tsx`, `cli-brand-icons.tsx`, `AttachmentImagePreviewDialog.tsx`, `home/UserInputMessage.tsx`, `home/ConversationComposer.tsx`) and `next/dynamic` in 4 — all trivially replaceable.

Non-server-component `.tsx` files under `src/app`/`src/components` that lack `"use client"` are mostly leaf presentational components pulled into client trees (all of `src/components/ui/*`, `src/components/settings/*`, most of `src/components/home/*`) — they're not real RSCs.

---

## 10. Load-bearing issues for the split

Ordered by how much they'll hurt.

**A. The event bus is same-process memory.** `src/server/events/named-events.ts` (500-entry ring, module-level) and `src/server/events/live-updates.ts` (module-level listener `Set` + version counter) are the entire real-time backbone. `emitNamedEvent` is called from ~everywhere in `src/server/`. If the runner is one process this is fine — but it means **the runner must be a single process**: you cannot horizontally scale it, and the Next server cannot be a separate process from the supervisor without losing every SSE frame. Today the Next server *is* the event bus owner; the bridge (7800) is separate and its state is polled into snapshots rather than pushed.

**B. `src/runtime/http/server.ts` cannot stream.** `writeFetchResponse` (lines 72–79) does `Buffer.from(await fetchResponse.arrayBuffer())` before `response.end()`. For `/api/events` and `/api/terminals/:id/stream` — which return never-ending `ReadableStream`s — this buffers forever. The standalone runner server therefore **does not currently support either SSE endpoint**, even though both are registered in the registry. This must be fixed (pipe the web stream to the `ServerResponse`, flush headers early, disable Nagle) before the runner can be the real server.

**C. Auth is cookie + same-origin; a remote frontend is cross-origin.** `enforceSameOrigin: true` on every mutation, `SameSite=Lax` cookies, and zero CORS headers mean an Electron/Tauri/mobile app on a different origin cannot authenticate today. The VSCode extension sidesteps this by proxying through the extension host with an explicit `sessionCookie` (`src/vscode-extension/bridge.ts`). For multi-runner you'll want a bearer-token scheme (the pairing tokens in `auth_pair_tokens` are the natural seed) plus explicit CORS + per-runner credential storage on the client.

**D. Multi-runner is not modeled anywhere.** `RuntimeAPIs` (`src/runtime-api/types.ts`) is a flat singleton with no runner identity. `web.ts` has a single `baseUrl`. The bootstrap payload, all the Manager singletons in `src/app/home/`, the SSE cursor (`initialLastEventId`), and the snapshot checksum cache are all implicitly scoped to "the one server". Adding N runners means: runner id on every entity key, one `EventSource` + one `lastEventId` cursor per runner, per-runner query cache keys, and per-runner auth.

**E. Filesystem-coupled APIs assume the client can see the runner's disk.** `/api/fs`, `/api/fs/files`, `/api/attachments`, `/api/projects/memory`, `/api/git`, project paths in conversation creation, and the JSONL worker streams under `<projectPath>/.omniharness/run-data/` are all runner-local paths surfaced raw to the UI (`FolderPickerDialog.tsx`, `FileAttachmentPickerDialog.tsx`, `FileViewerPanel.tsx`, `ComposerContainer.tsx`). Electron's `native.chooseFolder()` returns a *local* path, which is wrong once the runner is remote. This needs an explicit "paths belong to the runner, not the client" model.

**F. Process-object singletons.** `process.__omniHarnessTerminalManager`, `process.__omniHarnessDbStates`, `process.__omniHarnessApiSessionCache`, `liveProcesses` in `session-providers/process-store.ts`, plus `AgentRuntimeManager`'s maps. All fine for a single runner process; all break under any multi-process runner. Also note `getTerminalManager()` registers `process.once('exit'|'SIGTERM'|'SIGINT')` — a runner needs one owner of shutdown, not per-module handlers.

**G. `src/server/` imports leak into client components.** Only 5 files (§6), all type-only except `review-preferences.ts` (which exports a zod schema — a real runtime import in `PlanningReviewControls.tsx` and `PlanningArtifactsPanel.tsx`). Small but must be resolved before the frontend can build without `src/server/` on disk.

**H. The 29 direct-`fetch` call sites** (§2d) are the actual bulk of the work. Until `useHomeMutations.ts` (1,269 lines), `useHomeQueries.ts`, `LiveEventConnectionManager.ts`, `WorkerEntriesManager.ts`, `GitWorkspaceManager.ts`, `ProjectMemoryPanelManager.ts`, `ConversationNotificationManager.ts` and the dialog components route through `RuntimeAPIs`, hardcoded `/api/...` relative URLs pin the frontend to same-origin.

**I. Web push binds notifications to a browser origin.** VAPID keys live in the runner's `settings` table; `notification_subscriptions` are browser push endpoints; `public/sw.js` is a service worker. A native/mobile shell needs a different notification path (the `RuntimeAPIs.native.notify` hook exists for Electron already).

**J. Two servers must be co-located today.** The bridge (7800) and the Next server share the filesystem (JSONL run-data under project paths, CLI homes, credential profiles, `.omniharness/` lockfiles) and `process.cwd()` via `getAppRoot()`. They are one logical runner, not two independently deployable things.

**K. Dev-only remote-control surface.** `src/server/restart-control.ts` + `scripts/remote-restart.ts` + `OMNIHARNESS_REMOTE_RESTART_TOKEN`/`_PASSWORD`/`_HOST`/`_PORT` implement an out-of-band restart channel writing to `.omniharness/remote-restart.pid.json`. Worth deciding whether that becomes a runner-management API or gets dropped.

---

## Suggested target shape (given what exists)

```
packages/shared        <- src/lib/* + entries-types.ts + review-preferences.ts
                          + app/home/types.ts + runtime-api/types.ts + shared/locales
packages/runner        <- src/server/* + src/runtime/* + instrumentation logic
                          entry = fixed src/runtime/http/server.ts (streaming!)
                          absorbs scripts/agent-runtime.ts as an internal subsystem
packages/interface     <- src/ui + src/components + src/app/home + src/runtime-api
                          Vite SPA; src/ui/render-web.tsx is already the entry
apps/web (optional)    <- thin Next wrapper, or delete Next entirely
apps/electron          <- exists; switch from in-process runtime to remote client
apps/vscode            <- exists; already remote-capable
```

The three concrete blockers to unblock first, in order: **(B)** make the standalone server stream, **(C)** token auth + CORS, **(D)** runner identity in `RuntimeAPIs`. Everything else is mechanical.

SSE endpoints (2)\n\n1. **`GET /api/events`** — `src/runtime/http/routes/events.ts:814-958`. The central live-state stream. Frames: `update` (full snapshot JSON), typed named-event kinds, `stream.resync_required`, `update_error`, `: heartbeat`. Every frame carries `id:`; resume via `Last-Event-ID` header or `?lastEventId=`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1[&persisted=1]`, anchor id returned in `x-omni-last-event-id`, plus `x-omni-snapshot-checksum`.\n2. **`GET /api/terminals/:id/stream`** — `src/runtime/http/routes/terminals.ts:93-150`. PTY output; also resumable via `Last-Event-ID`. Header comment explicitly says \"SSE + POST because this runtime has no websocket server.\"\n\n### 2c. Client-side transport layer (already abstracted)\n\n- `src/runtime-api/types.ts` — the `RuntimeAPIs` interface (bootstrap, events.open/fetchLog, conversations, workers, settings, optional `native`, optional `editor`)\n- `src/runtime-api/web.ts` — **already supports `baseUrl` and injectable `fetchImpl`/`EventSourceImpl`** (`WebRuntimeApiOptions`). This is your remote-runner client, ~90% done.\n- `src/runtime-api/electron.ts` — reuses web adapter + preload IPC for native\n- `src/runtime-api/vscode.ts` — `postMessage` bridge; extension host proxies HTTP and SSE to a configured `serverUrl` (default `http://localhost:3035`)\n- `src/runtime-api/provider.tsx` — React context (`useRuntimeAPIs()`)\n\n### 2d. Direct `fetch()` calls that bypass `RuntimeAPIs` (the migration debt)\n\n29 files still call `/api/...` directly.
